### **Important Note**

Where manufacturer’s name and / or numbers are mentioned, they are purely for reference purposes only and not to indicate source of manufacture or any connection in the course of trade with the manufacturer’s name. Every effort has been made to ensure accuracy and compatibility, but there is a responsibility between seller and purchaser to be satisfied that the product is wholly appropriate for the purpose of its intended use when reasonably used. If in any doubt for fitment and maintenance information, please refer to the vehicle manufacturers recommendations.

Property in and title to goods supplied by the seller shall not pass to the purchaser until the price has been paid in full but any risk in respect of such goods shall pass to the purchaser upon the shipment of the goods ex Bike Alert’s warehouse.

### **Trading Terms**

The risk of loss, destruction or damage to Goods passes to Customers on collection. The property of any such goods shall not pass to the Customer until the Company has received payment in full of the price of the goods, the price of any other goods supplied by the Company and any other monies due on any account that is overdue.  All Standard Term & Conditions (which are available on request) apply to all orders.

### **Hiflofiltro Oil Filters Europe**

30 Optima Park, Thames Road, Crayford, Kent, DA1 4QX, United Kingdom Tel: +44 (0) 1322 526 236  Fax: +44 (0) 1322 528 487  email: sales@bikealert.com

### **Warranty**

Filters manufactured by Hiflofiltro are warranted to be free from defects in material and workmanship and to be suitable for the application and use as originally intended by the vehicle OEM Manufacturer.  Service and replacement intervals must be according to the instructions of the engine’s OEM Manufacturer.  Any filter proven defective during the engine or equipment manufacturer’s recommended service intervals and vehicle’s intended use will be replaced at no charge. In the event of an engine or equipment failure directly caused by a defective Hiflofiltro filter, which was properly installed and changed following the engine or equipment manufacturer’s recommended service intervals and during the normal use of the vehicle, Hiflofiltro will repair the damaged engine or equipment. Hiflofiltro makes no other warranty of any kind, whether expressed or implied and do not take any responsibility upon eventual, incidental and/or sequential damages involved in the use of their products. Claims for engine or equipment repairs provided under this warranty must be submitted within 30 days after discovery of damage. Hiflofiltro Technical Services Department reserves the right to examine the engine or equipment and filter to determine the amount of damage and whether it was caused by a defective Hiflofiltro filter. This warranty gives you specific legal rights.  This warranty does not affect your statutory rights. Engine and equipment manufacturer’s warranties remain in effect when Hiflofiltro filters are used.

## Pagina 3

# **Product Range**

## OE Replacement Oil Filters

Hiflofiltro is the world’s first and only TÜV approved motorcycle oil filter. Manufactured using the best materials available, all Hiflofiltro oil filters are tested and certified to meet or exceed original equipment performance levels. Available for street, scooter, motocross, enduro, ATV, UTV, marine outboard, and snowmobile models.

Leaf spring

**Heavy-duty steel canister Pressure relief valve** Four-point spot welded for extra safety and strength

**Highest quality filtration media** Matched to each specific application, designed to work with all oil types, mineral and synthetic

**Metal end caps** Pressed for accuracy and strength

**Heavy-duty steel base plate**

**Pre-lubricated gasket** Compression tested to ensure strong, long-lasting seal

**Centre core** Spot welded for additional strength

**Anti-drain valve**

## “RC” High Performance Oil Filters

### **Utimate filtration for both track and street**

Developed and manufactured to very high specifications to exceed the demands of modern racing engines and extreme racing conditions, the Hiflofiltro RC range provides numerous advantages for both road user and track racer.

- 17mm cross drilled nut enabling easy installation, removal, and lock wiring for race use

- Advanced high performance tri-fiber filtration media for optimum filtration, high capacity, and improved oil flow. Capable of capturing particles as small as 5 microns.

- Tough heavy duty steel canister capable of withstanding extreme pressure

- Special fibre or rubber coated relief valve for perfect sealing

- Compatible with all oil types, mineral and synthetic

2

## Pagina 4

# OE Replacement Air Filters

Hiflofiltro Air filters are manufactured to very high standards to be a direct replacement for original equipment filters. Best quality Hiflo filtering media, specially developed to protect the modern Japanese high performance engines.

For BMW models and other large capacity European motorcycles, Hiflofiltro is exclusively using filtration media which is made in Germany, for perfect OE compatibility, superior engine protection and top performance.

# Dual-Stage Foam Air Filters

## **For motocross, enduro, ATV, and scooter**

Constructed from two layers of open pore foam to provide excellent airflow and maximum filtration. The coarse outer layer traps larger particles of dirt and water, keeping the fine inner layer free to trap fine dust particles.

Hiflofiltro dual-stage foam filters are designed to be cleaned and re-used over and over again. The two foam layers are flame laminated together to provide a durable, permanent bond. The filter is constructed from pieces of dual-layer foam bonded together, using a specially formed adhesive that is unaffected by repeat cleaning and oiling cycles.

Developed and made in the Netherlands.

COARSE FOAM **TRAPS LARGE PARTICLES**

FINE FOAM

**TRAPS SMALL PARTICLES**

## Pagina 5

# The Hiflofiltro Difference

## **Manufactured by an experienced, TÜV certified production facility**

The Hiflofiltro-Thai Yang Kitpaisan factory was founded in 1955 and has been making filters for OE manufacturers since 1963. The facility’s quality management and production systems are certified by TÜV Rheinland under the ISO/TS 16949:2009 standard. The customer list includes automotive giants Denso, Toyota, Ford, Mazda, Suzuki, Nissan, Isuzu Hino, Hitachi and many others.

## **Assembled using only the best materials**

To achieve consistently high levels of quality, only premium materials from well known suppliers are used. Steel is sourced from Japanese manufacturers Toyota Tsusho and NKK, glue from Henkel in Germany, filter media from Ahlstrom in Finland, Hollingsworth & Vose in USA, and Awa Paper in Japan.

## **World class manufacturing processes**

With decades of experience, constant research and development, and modern production facilities, the best manufacturing processes in the industry are used to maintain our extreme standards of quality. Every filter goes through 16 individual quality control checks before it leaves the factory, ensuring that every Hiflofiltro oil filter provides maximum engine protection.

## **Intelligent design**

The first rule of a Hiflofiltro oil filter is that its specification and performance must meet or exceed the OE part it will replace. Only by maximising efficiency, flow rate, and capacity can the most suitable replacement oil filter be created.

4

**www.hiflo.com**

## Pagina 6

# **Every filter is tested and certified to meet or exceed OEM specifications**

In order to pass the TÜV of south Germany certification, every filter must be tested against the OE filter it is intended to replace. The test includes key categories such as filtration efficiency, pressure drop, bypass valve opening pressure, and burst strength. Furthermore, the production and quality control are routinely monitored by TUV. It is the only standard covering motorcycle oil filters, and to date, Hiflofiltro is the only TÜV approved filter.

# **Produced for private brands and European motorcycle manufacturers**

In addition to being sold under the Hiflofiltro name, Hiflofiltro is supplied to other well-known filter brands, and is installed by several European, American, Japanese automotive and motorcycle manufacturers as an OEM part.

# **World’s best-selling aftermarket motorcycle filter**

An oil filter may be a small and inexpensive component, but the engine it protects is anything but. Trust in reliability is an important part of selecting an oil filter, which is why Hiflofiltro sells more oil filters worldwide than any other aftermarket brand.

# **Extensive product range**

Hiflofiltro is the most complete filter range in the aftermarket, with applications for almost every motorcycle, scooter, and ATV that uses an oil filter.

# **Leading distributor network**

With an international network of leading distributors in over 70 countries worldwide, it is always possible to find an official supplier.

# **Availability**

Comprehensive stocks are always kept at the Hiflofiltro distribution centres in London UK and Atlanta USA, ready for immediate delivery. With more than two million filters in stock at any given time, and a fill rate of 99.9%, supply is never a problem.

# **OE Warranty and product liability insurance**

In view of its high quality of manufacture and certifications, the use of Hiflofiltro does not invalidate any European and American statutory OE warranties.

All Hiflofiltro products are covered by international product liability insurance.

5
